Motivation: 任务型对话代理在真实场景中脆弱，用户非合作行为下尤甚；现有function-calling基准偏重成功、合作型交互，对对抗性对话轨迹覆盖不足，导致训练鲁棒代理的数据资源匮乏。

Method: 提出ToolRACER合成数据生成管线，协调用户、助手与工具三类仿真模型生成并校验多轮交互；据此构建ToolRACERBench多轮对话基准，覆盖6个领域、55种人格，生成并经校验的5.6K对话轨迹，其中约66%含易失败场景，并注入对抗行为以得到贴近真实的鲁棒交互轨迹。

Result: 在内部基准及τ²-bench、BFCLv3、ACEBench等function calling基准上评估。在ToolRACERBench上训练的模型提升了τ²-bench与ACEBench的端到端agentic准确率；在小型语言模型的agent能力任务中，与域内数据集混合训练时取得显著增益。

Conclusion: 以对抗行为注入与多模型协同校验为核心的合成数据管线，可有效弥补现有基准对非合作对话覆盖的不足，提升任务型对话代理的准确率与鲁棒性，尤其利于小型语言模型的agent能力训练。

Abstract: Task-oriented conversational agents remain fragile under real world conversation scenarios as they rarely follow a predictable script, especially when users exhibit non-cooperative behavior. Existing function-calling benchmarks often emphasize successful, cooperative interactions and underrepresent adversarial conversation trajectories, thereby limiting the training resources available for developing robust agents. We present ToolRACER, a synthetic data generation pipeline that coordinates user, assistant and tool emulation models to generate and validated multi-turn interactions between a user and an agent. Using \sysn, we construct ToolRACERBench a robust multi-turn conversation benchmark spanning six domains, ranging over 55 varied personas, generating a validated corpus of 5.6K conversation trajectories, with approximately 66\% of conversations containing failure-prone conversation scenarios. We inject adversarial behaviors, producing validated conversational interaction trajectories that capture realistic, robust scenarios. We evaluate models trained on ToolRACERBench against internal benchmarks, as well as on function calling benchmarks such as $τ^2$-bench, BFCLv3 and ACEBench to evaluate agentic accuracy and robustness. Models trained on ToolRACERBench improve end to end agentic accuracy across $τ^2$-bench and ACEBench, demonstrating significant gains when mixed with in-domain dataset in small language models for agent capability tasks.

</details>


### [174] [Multi-Objective Aligned Small Language Model Framework for SUD Patient Dialogue Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.09209)
*Thushara Manjari Naduvilakandy,Hyeju Jang,Mohammad Al Hasan*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该论文提出一个认知驱动的框架，用于生成物质使用障碍（SUD）患者对话，通过显式建模潜在认知成分并训练小语言模型（SLM），在降低算力与隐私成本的同时提升认知连贯性与临床真实性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM虽能生成流畅文本，但在SUD咨询中难以产生认知连贯、临床真实的患者回应，尤其受伦理与数据稀缺限制。同时，前沿大模型在医疗部署中面临计算成本高、延迟大、隐私风险和资源受限环境难以落地等问题，因此需要认知对齐的小语言模型。

Method: 提出两阶段流水线：认知成分检测，以及认知成分对齐的对话生成。为使小模型有效学习，结合高容量教师模型的知识蒸馏、基于人工标注的偏好优化，以及注意力引导的奖励塑形。

Result: 使用BERTScore、ROUGE、METEOR、BLEU等自动指标，以及LLM-as-judge命中指标，并对照人类与教师模型参考进行评估；结果显示认知信息驱动的微调在认知实现和对齐方面显著优于通用指令微调基线和心理健康领域专用SLM，对开放式认知成分提升尤为明显。

Conclusion: 认知驱动的建模与对齐能有效提升SUD患者对话生成质量，并为资源受限医疗场景提供可部署的SLM方案。

Abstract: Substance Use Disorder (SUD) counseling requires patient responses that reflect underlying cognitive states such as beliefs, coping strategies, and readiness for change. Although large language models (LLMs) can generate fluent text, they often fail to produce cognitively coherent and clinically realistic patient behavior, especially under ethical and data-scarce clinical settings. Moreover, deploying frontier-scale LLMs in healthcare applications presents practical challenges including high computational cost, latency, privacy concerns, and limited deployability in resource-constrained environments, motivating the need for cognitively aligned small language models (SLMs). We propose a cognitively grounded framework for SUD patient dialogue generation that explicitly models and aligns latent cognitive components with patient histories and counselor questions. Our pipeline consists of two stages: cognitive component detection and cognitive component-aligned dialogue generation. To enable effective learning with smaller models, we combine knowledge distillation from high-capacity teacher models, preference optimization from human-annotations, and attention-guided reward shaping. Extensive evaluations using automatic scores like BERTScore, ROUGE, METEOR and BLEU, and LLM-as-judge hit-metrics against both human and teacher-model references show that cognitively informed fine-tuning substantially improves cognitive realization and alignment over a generic instruction-tuned baselines and mental health domain specific SLMs, with particularly strong gains for open-ended cognitive components.

</details>


### [175] [Dialect-Robust Speech Language Models with Synthetic Pseudo-Dialect Augmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.09321)
*Shunsuke Mitsumori,Tomoya Mizumoto,Yusuke Fujita*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出零真实方言语音的伪方言语音合成增强方法，通过LLM生成方言文本再经标准语TTS转换，并引入中间标准文本预测，有效提升方言语音理解性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 语音语言模型在方言上性能下降，主因是方言数据稀缺；传统TTS增强仍需一定量真实方言语音，难以覆盖多样方言。

Method: 用LLM生成方言文本，经标准语TTS模型转换为伪方言语音，实现零真实方言语音增强；同时在训练中引入中间标准文本预测，作为语义归一化。

Result: 在日语、德语、中文方言的方言到英语语音翻译任务上，伪方言增强较合成标准语音基线提升：日语25.38→26.24，德语31.57→32.47；加入中间标准文本预测后进一步提升，日语达28.26，中文11.67→16.37。

Conclusion: 该方法无需各方言特定语音资源即可扩展至多种语言，能有效缓解方言理解中的数据稀缺与语义鸿沟问题。

Abstract: Speech Language Model (SLM) performance often degrades on dialects due to data scarcity. Conventional text-to-speech (TTS) augmentation struggles to cover diverse dialects as it requires a certain amount of real dialect speech. We propose synthesizing pseudo-dialect speech by converting LLM-generated dialect text via a standard-language TTS model, requiring zero real dialect speech. Additionally, we introduce intermediate standard-text prediction during training, acting as semantic normalization for downstream tasks. We evaluate dialect understanding via dialect-to-English speech translation across Japanese, German, and Chinese dialects. Compared to synthetic standard speech baselines, pseudo-dialect augmentation improves scores for Japanese (from 25.38 to 26.24) and German (from 31.57 to 32.47). Furthermore, the intermediate standard-text prediction effectively bridges the semantic gap, boosting performance to 28.26 for Japanese and from 11.67 to 16.37 for Chinese. These results suggest that our approach scales to various languages without requiring speech resources specific to each dialect.

</details>


### [176] [OnlineQAT: On-Policy Distillation for Ultra-Low-Bit Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.09346)
*Wenjun Wang,Heng Li,Yanggan Gu,Hongxia Yang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出 OnlineQAT 两阶段框架，通过块级 QAT 获得低比特初始化，再利用学生自生成回复进行在线策略蒸馏，缓解量化误差导致的状态偏移问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 QAT 恢复阶段多在固定补全或教师生成答案上优化，而部署时的量化模型以自身生成的 prefix 为条件，量化误差会将模型推向离线恢复数据未覆盖的状态。

Method: 两阶段框架：第一阶段通过 block-wise QAT 获得可用的低比特初始化；第二阶段在学生自生成回复上执行 on-policy distillation (OPD)，在每个访问到的 prefix 处由冻结的全精度教师提供采样的 reverse-KL 训练信号。

Result: 在 Qwen3-1.7B 上取得对比方法中最佳平均表现：W3A16 为 57.28，W2A16 为 32.52，分别较 ReasoningQAT 提升 2.90 和 0.44 分。

Conclusion: 学生访问到的状态可提供超越固定补全训练的有效恢复信号，在 3 比特场景下尤为明显。

Abstract: Quantization-aware training (QAT) can recover much of the accuracy lost when large language models are compressed below four bits. Existing re- covery stages, however, are commonly optimized on fixed completions or teacher-generated answers, whereas the deployed quantized model condi- tions on prefixes generated by itself. Quantization errors can therefore move the model into states that are absent from offline recovery data. We introduce OnlineQAT, a two-stage framework that first obtains a usable low-bit initialization through block-wise QAT and then performs on-policy distillation (OPD) on student-generated responses. At each visited pre- fix, a frozen full-precision teacher provides a sampled reverse-KL training signal. On Qwen3-1.7B, OnlineQAT obtains the best average among the compared quantized methods: 57.28 at W3A16 and 32.52 at W2A16, im- proving over ReasoningQAT by 2.90 and 0.44 points, respectively. The results suggest that student-visited states provide a useful recovery signal beyond fixed-completion training, particularly at three bits.

</details>


### [177] [Expert Coupling in MoE Pretraining: Reducing All-to-All Overhead with Correlated Placement and Token Shuffling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.09372)
*Radha Gulhane,Quentin Anthony,Beren Millidge*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该论文发现MoE预训练早期路由器已形成专家选择的层内与跨层相关性，并据此提出相关专家放置与token shuffling两种策略，在不改变路由决策与专家参数的前提下，将all-to-all通信时间降低1.16–2.63倍、端到端步时最多加速1.41倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在专家并行（EP）下，MoE每层的all-to-all集合通信开销巨大：在单节点8张MI300X、EP32配置下，top-2路由占训练步时45%，top-6路由高达60%。核心痛点是跨GPU、跨节点的token-expert分发通信成为训练瓶颈，亟需在不改变模型语义的前提下减少通信量。

Method: 作者观察到预训练早期路由器已将token以相关模式分配给专家：层内0.8%的专家对共同承载42%的token选择，且某层选中的专家可预测下一层所选专家。基于此提出两种方法：(1) 相关专家放置，将常被共同选中的专家放到同一GPU上，配合使每个token对每个GPU只发送一次的dispatcher，最多消除58%的分发行；(2) token shuffling，在序列并行将token分片至EP组时，于attention后的reduce-scatter阶段将token移动到预测持有其下一层专家的GPU上，单节点内使本地服务比例从12.5%提升至59%。

Result: 在Megatron-LM上，EP度数8至64、top-2与top-6路由下，两种方法将all-to-all时间降低1.16–2.63倍，端到端步时最多加速1.41倍，且不改变模型的路由决策或专家参数。

Conclusion: 利用MoE路由器天然习得的token-expert分配相关性，可在不改动模型语义的前提下显著降低专家并行的通信开销，为大规模MoE训练提供了一种通用且无损的通信优化途径。

Abstract: Mixture-of-Experts (MoE) layers replace the feed-forward block of a Transformer with E expert networks, and each token is routed to k of these experts. Under expert parallelism (EP) the experts are distributed across GPUs, and every MoE layer runs all-to-all collectives in the forward and backward passes to dispatch tokens to their experts and then combine the results. On a cluster with 8 AMD Instinct MI300X GPUs per node, these collectives can take 45% of the training step at EP32 with top-2 routing and 60% with top-6 routing. We find that early in pretraining routers have already learned to assign tokens to experts in correlated patterns, both within a layer and across layers. At top-2, 0.8% of the expert pairs in a layer are selected together by 42% of tokens, and the experts a token selects at one layer predict the experts it selects at the next layer. We use these correlations to keep more token--expert assignments on the token's own GPU, which reduces communication across GPUs and across nodes. Correlated expert placement puts experts that are often selected together on the same GPU. Combined with a dispatcher that sends each token to each GPU once, it removes up to 58% of dispatched rows. Token shuffling applies when sequence parallelism shards tokens across the EP group. It moves each token to the GPU predicted to hold its next-layer experts during the reduce-scatter that follows attention. On one node this raises the share of token--expert assignments served on the token's GPU from 12.5% to 59%. In Megatron-LM, across EP degrees from 8 to 64 with top-2 and top-6 routing, the two methods reduce all-to-all time by 1.16-2.63X and end-to-end step time by up to 1.41X. Neither method changes the models' underlying routing decisions or expert parameters.

</details>


### [178] [The Persona Hierarchy Model: Understanding Contextual Generalization in Fine-Tuning LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.09384)
*Jiachen Zhao,Zhengxuan Wu,David Bau,Weiyan Shi*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出Persona Hierarchy Model解释微调泛化的情境依赖性：共享默认人格跨情境影响行为，情境局部人格的改变更具情境特异性；据此提出人格保持正则化(PPR)以抑制不良泛化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 语言模型常在固定情境（如通用系统提示、人格设定或领域指令）下微调，但习得行为有时局限于该情境，有时又泛化到未见情境，其机制缺乏解释。作者希望揭示这种泛化差异的成因，并据此实现对非预期泛化的控制以改进对齐。

Method: 提出Persona Hierarchy Model，区分共享默认人格与情境局部人格；在4种行为、15个训练情境下微调120个模型，计算泛化狭窄度与训练情境人格同默认人格相似度的相关性（Qwen3-4B上Pearson r=0.72）；检验默认情境预训练对后续训练泛化的拓宽作用；将情境响应与默认人格响应对齐；并提出人格保持正则化(PPR)，在强化学习中约束非预期的情境泛化。

Result: 泛化狭窄度与训练情境人格和默认人格的相似度显著正相关（r=0.72）；先前的默认情境微调可拓宽后续其他情境训练的泛化；将情境响应对齐默认人格响应效果更强；RL中PPR将奖励黑客从42–55%降至所有评估提示下至多0.2%，同时保留准确率提升。

Conclusion: 结果支持Persona Hierarchy Model作为情境泛化的解释框架，并为未来控制非预期泛化、提升LLM对齐提供了可行路径。

Abstract: Language models are routinely fine-tuned under a fixed context, such as a generic system prompt, persona or domain-specific instruction, yet the learned behavior sometimes stays confined to that context and sometimes broadly generalizes to unseen contexts. We propose the Persona Hierarchy Model to explain this: a shared default persona influences behavior across contexts. Under this model, fine-tuning that modifies the shared persona promotes broader transfer, whereas changes to local personas remain more context-specific. Across 120 fine-tuned models spanning four behaviors and 15 training contexts, generalization narrowness positively correlates with the similarity between the training context's persona and the default persona (Pearson's r = 0.72 for Qwen3-4B). Prior fine-tuning under the default context can broaden generalization in subsequent training under other contexts. Aligning contextual responses with default-persona responses produces stronger effects. Finally, we propose persona-preserving regularization (PPR) to confine undesired contextual generalization. In RL, PPR cuts reward hacking from 42-55% to at most 0.2% under every evaluated prompt while retaining accuracy gains. These results support the Persona Hierarchy Model as an explanation for contextual generalization and can motivate future controls on unintended generalization for better alignment of LLMs.

</details>


### [179] [ARCS: Towards Precise Text-to-SQL via Structured Disambiguation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.09396)
*Yihao Hu,Yanlin Feng,Naoki Otani,Nikita Bhutani*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 论文提出"结构化消歧"新范式,并构建首个真实场景文本到SQL歧义基准ARCS,实验显示现有模型在歧义场景下性能大幅下降。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 文本到SQL系统走向真实部署时,用户问题中的歧义成为主要错误来源。这类歧义往往是细微的、依赖领域或数据的,会悄然使输出偏离用户真实意图。传统上通过对话式澄清解决歧义,但效率低、认知负担重,且与实际用户工作流不匹配。

Method: 提出结构化消歧范式:通过显式、受约束的交互而非自由对话来消解歧义。为此构建ARCS(面向SQL的歧义消解语料库),这是首个文本到SQL基准,具有真实数据库上自然出现的无约束歧义,并完整标注了所有有效歧义点、释义以及SQL查询。

Result: 实验表明歧义场景下文本到SQL仍具挑战性:gpt-6-sol端到端执行准确率仅为51%,开源模型无一超过27%。

Conclusion: 现有模型难以应对真实歧义;结构化消歧为文本到SQL的歧义处理提供了新方向,ARCS基准为相关研究提供了评测基础。

Abstract: As text-to-SQL systems move beyond demonstrations toward real-world deployment, ambiguity in user questions becomes a primary source of errors. Such ambiguities are often subtle, domain- or data-specific, and can silently cause system outputs to deviate from the user's true intent. Ambiguity is traditionally addressed through conversational clarification, which is often inefficient, cognitively demanding, and poorly aligned with real-world user workflows. We propose structured disambiguation, a new paradigm in which ambiguity is resolved through explicit, constrained interactions rather than free-form dialogue. We construct ARCS (Ambiguity Resolution Corpus for SQL), the first text-to-SQL benchmark featuring naturally occurring, unconstrained ambiguities over real-world databases, with complete annotations of all valid ambiguity points, interpretations, and SQL queries. Experimental results show that text-to-SQL remains challenging in the presence of ambiguity: gpt-6-sol achieves only 51% end-to-end execution accuracy, and no open-source model exceeds 27%.

</details>


### [180] [Arctic Questions, Missing Answers: A Dataset and Benchmark for LLM Abstention in Arctic Science](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.09446)
*Benjamin Wilcox,Dawei Gao,Pradeeban Kathiravelu,Douglas Causey,Kewei Sha,Yunhe Feng*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 论文提出ArcticQA数据集与ArcticAbstain基准，评估大模型在答案缺失时是否应弃答，发现弃答率差异大且对答案可用性敏感度有限。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在科学多选题中，当无正确选项时大模型应弃答；但仅看高频弃答不足以证明模型对答案是否可用的敏感性，需要成对比较来区分基线弃答倾向与真正的动态响应。

Method: 构建ArcticQA数据集，含194道源自北极基础研究的题目，并用自动化方法校验答案支持与干扰项矛盾。进一步设计ArcticAbstain成对基准，对比"有答案"与"无答案"条件（后者将正确答案替换为干扰项），两种条件均提供显式弃答选项。评估Gemini、Claude和ChatGPT三大系列共八个模型，采用高推理强度，每种条件三次试验，共记录9,312条回复。

Result: 在有答案条件下，各模型弃答率在0.0%至63.0%之间；替换正确答案后，弃答率平均上升5.05个百分点。

Conclusion: 结果揭示了显著的基线差异，表明必须联合评估弃答的频率与响应性；仅凭弃答率无法反映模型对答案可用性的真实敏感度。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) should abstain from scientific multiple-choice questions when no option is valid, but frequent abstention alone does not demonstrate sensitivity to answer availability. We introduce ArcticQA, a dataset of 194 questions derived from primary Arctic research, with automated checks of answer support and distractor contradiction against source evidence. We further develop ArcticAbstain, a paired benchmark comparing answer-present and answer-absent conditions, with the correct answer replaced by a distractor in the latter and an explicit abstention option in both. We evaluate eight models from the Gemini, Claude, and ChatGPT families at high reasoning effort, with three trials per condition, yielding 9,312 recorded responses. Answer-present abstention rates range from 0.0% to 63.0%, whereas replacing the correct answer increases abstention by 5.05 percentage points on average. These findings highlight substantial baseline differences and the need to evaluate abstention frequency and responsiveness jointly. The dataset and benchmark are available at https://github.com/BenWilcox8/arctic-qa.

</details>


### [181] [Right Number, Wrong State? Measuring Cross-Jurisdiction Substitution in LLM Recall of State Policy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.09458)
*Jiayu Feng*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该研究用最小集合设计（仅变更辖区、固定措辞）检验LLM在美国51个辖区、3项Medicaid收入资格数值上的"跨州替换"错误。两个模型对153个条目中分别有10和25个可复现地给出他州当前值。但归因脆弱：若把任何等于他州值的错误都算作替换，会比严格核查本州记录多出3–5倍，因为许多表面跨州答案实为本州值在其他口径或早年度的情形。跨辖区错误研究需完整的同州参照集。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM在回答州特定政策问题出错时，究竟是幻觉，还是返回了另一个州真实成立的值，此前缺乏清晰区分。作者希望用受控实验把"跨辖区混淆"这一错误类型从一般幻觉中分离出来并加以量化。

Method: 最小集合设计：问题措辞固定，仅辖区在50个州与哥伦比亚特区（51个辖区）间变化，涉及3个精确定义的Medicaid收入资格指标。金标准来自官方数据手册，并在102个抽查单元中有101个与独立来源一致。采用预注册协议，对Claude Sonnet 5.5与GPT-5.6 Sol各进行两次独立重复。

Result: 两个模型分别对153个条目中的10个和25个，可复现地给出另一州的当前值，且两次独立重复结果完全一致。但归因结论脆弱：若将任何等于他州值的错误答案都计为替换，得到的可复现替换数比逐项核查本州自身记录多3–5倍，因为许多表面上的跨州答案其实是本州值在另一统计口径下或来自更早年份。

Conclusion: 跨辖区错误的可靠归因需要完整的同州参照集（涵盖本州不同口径与历史年份的取值）；否则会显著高估跨州替换的发生率。作者将公开协议、金标准表和全部模型输出。

Abstract: When an LLM answers a state-specific policy question wrongly, it may be hallucinating, or it may be returning a real value that holds in another state. We test this with a minimal-set design: the question wording is fixed and only the jurisdiction varies, across the 50 U.S. states and the District of Columbia (51 jurisdictions) and three exactly defined Medicaid income-eligibility quantities. Gold values come from an official data book and agree with an independent source in 101 of 102 checked cells. Under a pre-registered protocol, Claude Sonnet 5.5 and GPT-5.6 Sol reproducibly give another state's current value, identical across two independent repeats, for 10 and 25 of 153 items. Attribution is fragile, however. Crediting any wrong answer that equals another state's value yields 3-5x more reproducible substitutions than checking every number in the asked state's own records, because many apparent cross-state answers are the asked state's own values under another convention or from an earlier year. Claims about cross-jurisdiction error need a complete same-state reference set. We will release the protocol, gold table, and all model outputs.

</details>


### [182] [BanglaRhet: Benchmarking Classical and Transformer Models for Rhetorical and Persuasion Detection in Bangla Political Speech](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.09464)
*Rohit Kumar Sen,Anik Chowdhury*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文构建孟加拉语政治话语修辞与说服技巧检测基准，使用BanglaRhet语料（30,289条）评估四种Transformer模型，BanglaBERT取得最佳效果，macro-F1分别为65.40%和66.46%，显著超越经典TF-IDF基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 孟加拉语NLP在情感分析和意见挖掘方面已有进展，但针对孟加拉语政治演讲中细粒度修辞形式与说服意图检测的Transformer模型系统性基准评测仍严重缺乏。政治话语常通过修辞与说服性语言构建叙事、影响舆论并动员受众，因此亟需建立相关基准。

Method: 使用BanglaRhet语料库（从公开政治新闻来源收集的30,289条人工标注孟加拉语政治演讲片段），构建两个有监督单标签分类任务：修辞技巧检测（对比、重复、夸张、隐喻、反问）与说服技巧检测（归咎、行动号召、团结呼吁、道德诉求、情感诉求、逻辑诉求）。评估四个Transformer模型（BanglaBERT、BanglaBERT-Base、SahajBERT、XLM-RoBERTa-Base），并与经典TF-IDF基线对比。

Result: BanglaBERT表现最佳：修辞技巧检测macro-F1为65.40%，说服技巧检测为66.46%，分别超出最优调参经典基线19.2和13.8个macro-F1点。类别层面分析显示，错误主要源于标签间语义重叠、比喻性语言和类别不平衡。

Conclusion: 该研究为孟加拉语修辞与说服感知的政治话语分析提供了初步基准，并指出未来需要上下文感知与多标签建模方法。

Abstract: Political discourse often uses rhetorical and persuasive language to frame narratives, influence public opinion, and mobilize audiences. While Bangla natural language processing has made progress in sentiment analysis and opinion mining, systematic benchmarking of transformer models for fine-grained rhetorical and persuasion technique detection in Bangla political speech remains largely underexplored. This paper presents a benchmark study of transformer-based models for detecting rhetorical form and persuasive intent in Bangla political discourse. Using BanglaRhet, a manually annotated corpus of 30,289 Bangla political speech segments collected from publicly available political news sources, we formulate two supervised single-label classification tasks: rhetorical technique detection (contrast, repetition, exaggeration, metaphor, rhetorical questions) and persuasion technique detection (blame assignment, call to action, unity call, moral, emotional, and logical appeals). We evaluate four transformer-based models, BanglaBERT, BanglaBERT-Base, SahajBERT, and XLM-RoBERTa-Base, against classical TF-IDF baselines. BanglaBERT achieves the highest performance, with 65.40% macro-F1 for rhetorical technique detection and 66.46% for persuasion technique detection, outperforming the best tuned classical baseline by 19.2 and 13.8 macro-F1 points, respectively. Class-level analysis indicates that errors are mainly associated with semantic overlap among labels, figurative language, and class imbalance. The results provide initial benchmark baselines for Bangla rhetorical and persuasion-aware political discourse analysis and highlight the need for context-aware and multi-label modeling.

</details>


### [183] [Goldsmith: Gold-Loss-Guided Definition Optimization with an Agentic Annotation Harness](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.09489)
*Yihan Li,Hanyi Zhang,Xiaoxi Jiang,Man Guo*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: Goldsmith 将少量专家标注的 gold set 转化为可训练的结构化标注定义，通过可执行结构化损失驱动 LLM 编辑进行文本梯度式修订，在提示优化与下游标注任务上均取得提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 许多标注项目在专家尚未形成稳定指南、也缺乏足够训练任务专用模型的标签时就已启动，因此需要一种方法利用稀缺的专家监督同时支撑任务定义学习与可扩展标注。

Method: Goldsmith 是一个 agentic pipeline：以少量专家标注的校准样本（gold set，代表预期任务边界）为输入，产出可复用的结构化标注定义，并把该定义视为可训练的文本对象。候选定义在相同 gold 样本上运行，并用可执行结构化损失评分；输出模式、格式、检索、修复、评判与人工复核均置于外部 harness 中。LLM 编辑器将损失最高的失败案例转化为文本梯度式修订，仅当测得损失下降时才接受该修订。检索、基于分数的路由与人工复核进一步与该定义结合用于下游标注。

Result: 在匹配评估协议下，Goldsmith 优于直接重写、OPRO、APE 和 PromptBreeder；所得定义在类型化 span、pair-level relation 以及 fixed-trigger event-argument 三类任务上，与检索、基于分数的路由和人工复核结合后，均改善了下游标注效果。

Conclusion: 结果表明，稀缺的专家监督既能支持任务定义学习，也能支持可扩展的标注。

Abstract: Many annotation projects begin before experts have a stable guideline or enough labels to train a task-specific model. We present Goldsmith, an agentic pipeline that turns a small gold set---expert-annotated calibration examples representing the intended task boundaries---into a reusable structured annotation definition. Goldsmith treats this definition as a trainable textual object. Candidate definitions are run on the same gold examples and scored with an executable structured loss, while the output schema, formatting, retrieval, repair, judging, and human review remain in an external harness. A large language model (LLM) editor converts the highest-loss failures into textual-gradient revisions, which are accepted only when the measured loss decreases. In prompt-optimization comparisons, Goldsmith improves over direct rewriting, OPRO, APE, and PromptBreeder under matched evaluation protocols. The resulting definition also improves downstream annotation when combined with retrieval, score-based routing, and human review across typed span, pair-level relation, and fixed-trigger event-argument tasks. These results show that scarce expert supervision can support both task-definition learning and scalable annotation.

</details>


### [184] [A Comparative Study of Evaluation Metrics for Long-Document Financial Narrative Summarization with Transformers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.09529)
*Nadhem Zmandar,Mo El-Haj,Paul Rayson*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文对多种摘要方法与不同预训练Transformer及抽取技术进行基准测试，提出新的BRUGE评分指标，并通过显著性检验与对抗分析验证结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 英国上市公司年报冗长（平均约80页），需有效的自动摘要方法；同时现有评估指标可能无法反映真实摘要能力，需要系统比较多种摘要方法及评估指标。

Method: 在Financial Narrative Summarisation (FNS 2020)数据集上，对多种预训练Transformer模型结合不同抽取技术进行基准测试；采用多种评估指标进行比较；提出新的BRUGE评分，即ROUGE-2与BERTscore的调和平均；并进行统计显著性检验及三种扰动方法下的对抗分析。

Result: 通过多指标比较揭示了不同评估指标的行为与适用性差异，发现部分指标不能真实反映摘要能力；BRUGE评分被提出用于综合衡量；统计显著性检验与对抗分析用于验证结果的稳健性。

Conclusion: 该研究为金融文本摘要提供了系统基准与评估视角，并提出BRUGE作为补充指标，同时通过统计与对抗分析增强结论可信度。

Abstract: There are more than 2,000 listed companies on the UK's London Stock Exchange, divided into 11 sectors who are required to communicate their financial results at least twice in a single financial year. UK annual reports are very lengthy documents with around 80 pages on average. In this study, we aim to benchmark a variety of summarisation methods on a set of different pre-trained transformers with different extraction techniques. In addition, we considered multiple evaluation metrics in order to investigate their differing behaviour and applicability on a dataset from the Financial Narrative Summarisation (FNS 2020) shared task, which is composed of annual reports published by firms listed on the London Stock Exchange and their corresponding summaries. We hypothesise that some evaluation metrics do not reflect true summarisation ability and propose a novel BRUGEscore metric, as the harmonic mean of ROUGE-2 and BERTscore. Finally, we perform a statistical significance test on our results to verify whether they are statistically robust, alongside an adversarial analysis task with three different corruption methods.

</details>


### [185] [RELATE: An Evaluation Framework for measuring Relational Orientation of Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.09569)
*Shivam Shukla,Jihye Kim,Shubham Gaur,Mahnaz Roshanaei,Magy Seif El-Nasr*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 论文提出"关系取向"（relational orientation）概念，区分AI情感支持中"向内向"（IF，把AI定位为用户持续支持来源）与"向外支架"（OS，鼓励现实人际连接）两类语言，并构建RELATE框架在句子层面测量多轮对话中的取向。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM被越来越多用于情感支持，人们担忧长期使用会使用户疏离现实人际关系。但现有评估主要关注回复的安全性、共情或有用性，忽视了一个关系性问题：模型把用户引向何处寻求持续支持。

Method: 以心理学与社会学文献为基础，形式化关系取向的分类体系；提出RELATE——一种基于人格（persona）条件化的框架，在句子层面测量多轮对话中的IF与OS语言。将76个求助情境（改编自自然发生的问题）与3种模拟用户风格配对，构成228个评估刺激；用7个LLM、每个对话6个助手轮次进行实验，共1,596个对话、69,194句助手发言；主要采用基于评分标准的LLM评判，并对子集使用第二评判者。

Result: 自动评估发现：第六轮助手发言中被标为IF的句子比例高于第一轮；对于犹豫、间接的模拟用户，被标为OS的句子比例明显低于明确寻求安慰的用户。

Conclusion: RELATE提供了可复现的框架和句子级信号，可用于审计和引导支持型LLM的关系取向。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) are increasingly used for emotional support, raising concern that sustained use may draw users away from their real-world relationships. Yet existing evaluations primarily focus on the safety, empathy, or helpfulness of responses, leaving under-examined a relational question: where does the model orient the user for continued support? To address this question, we introduce relational orientation, a property operationalized through two non-exclusive dimensions: inward-facing (IF) language, which positions the AI as the user's ongoing source of support, and outward-scaffolding (OS) language, which encourages real-world human connection. Grounded in psychological and sociological literature, we formalize a taxonomy of relational orientation and present RELATE, a persona-conditioned framework for measuring inward-facing and outward-scaffolding language at the sentence level in multi-turn dialogues. RELATE pairs 76 help-seeking situations adapted from naturally occurring questions with three simulated user styles, providing 228 evaluation stimuli. In our experiments, we evaluate seven LLMs using dialogues with six assistant turns each, yielding 1,596 dialogues and 69,194 assistant sentences. We assess these sentences using a primary rubric-based LLM judge and apply a secondary judge to a subset. Under automated evaluation, we find that the proportion of sentences labeled as IF is higher at the sixth assistant turn than at the first, while the proportion labeled as OS is substantially lower for hesitant, indirect simulated users than for explicit, reassurance-seeking users. RELATE provides a reproducible framework and a sentence-level signal for auditing and steering the relational orientation of supportive LLMs.

</details>


### [186] [How Do LLMs Change Predictions Under Negation?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.09571)
*Jongwook Yoon,Jongwon Lim,Sungjib Lim,Woojin Cho,Yohan Jo*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该论文通过机制分析揭示LLM否定处理依赖“抑制原答案+提升候选答案”的注意头与MLP神经元，指出人机差异是失败根源，并提出新训练目标减少否定错误。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 否定是人类语言的基本特征，但大语言模型（LLM）在处理否定时仍不可靠。作者希望通过机制层面的分析，理解模型否定失败的成因并针对性改进。

Method: 在自建的否定基准上评估近期开源与闭源LLM；对模型在否定下的内部机制进行机械化分析，定位专门负责否定的注意力头与MLP神经元；据此设计一种训练目标，要求原始预测越自信时答案偏好转移越大，并与标准微调基线对比。

Result: 37-71%的情况下模型在否定下重复相同答案（如对“What is not the capital of Spain?”仍答“Madrid”）；机制上，特定注意力头与MLP神经元联合实现否定：抑制原答案检索并提升答案类别内的偏好候选；与人类否定处理（利用原答案信息确定排除对象）形成对比；所提训练目标在减少否定失败的同时，对通用能力的退化小于标准微调基线。

Conclusion: 该研究展示机制分析如何揭示语言能力失败的原因，并指导针对底层局限的训练改进，从而提升LLM的否定处理可靠性。

Abstract: Negation is an essential feature of human language, yet large language models (LLMs) remain unreliable in processing it. We evaluate recent open-source and closed-source LLMs on our negation benchmark and find that, in 37-71% of cases, they repeat the same answer under negation (e.g., "Madrid" for "What is not the capital of Spain?"). To understand and address this brittleness, we mechanistically examine how models operate under negation. Our main finding is that specialized attention heads and MLP neurons jointly implement negation by (1) suppressing retrieval of the original answer (e.g., "Madrid") while (2) promoting a favored candidate within the answer category (e.g., "Paris"). This contrasts with accounts of human negation processing, in which information about the original answer helps to determine what should be excluded. Furthermore, we find that this difference from human processing is a key source of negation failures: the model's mechanism relies on suppressing the original answer rather than using it to determine what to exclude, so the model can repeat the original answer when suppression is too weak or when a bias toward particular answers prevents it from selecting an alternative. To address this weakness in the model's negation mechanism, we propose a training objective that requires larger shifts in answer preference for more confident original predictions, and show that it reduces negation failures with less degradation of general capabilities than standard fine-tuning baselines. Together, our results demonstrate how mechanistic analysis can reveal why a linguistic capability fails and guide training that targets the underlying limitation.

</details>


### [187] [Which Language Should a Skeleton Speak? Language Choices in Multilingual Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.09607)
*HyeonSeok Lim,SeungWoo Song,Inho Won,Hoyun Song,Jihyo Kim,KyungTae Lim*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该论文提出语言感知骨架探索框架LASEF，系统研究多语言数学推理中"推理骨架语言"的选择问题，发现英语骨架仅带来微弱平均优势，且并非普遍最优，骨架语言是依赖上下文的设计变量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于骨架的推理提示（skeleton-based reasoning prompting）是一种有前景的免训练结构化LLM推理方法，但既有工作大多默认英语中心（English-centric）设定，忽视了多语言场景下骨架语言选择的影响，缺乏系统性研究。

Method: 提出语言感知骨架探索框架LASEF，在数学基准、模型规模和多语言上开展实验；采用贪心解码、多轮采样评估（multi-rollout）、翻译消融（translation ablation）与跨基准验证相结合的方法，分析骨架语言效应。

Result: 英语骨架平均仅有小幅正向倾向，在较小模型和低资源语言上更明显；但经多重比较校正后，语言层面的增益大多不再显著，英语并非普遍最优。归纳出三类骨架语言效应模式：方向一致型、依赖评估与基准型、非对称负向型；这些效应无法仅用生成质量解释。

Conclusion: 骨架语言是依赖上下文的设计变量（context-dependent design variable），需要多层次探索，而非默认选择英语；相关资源已开源。

Abstract: Skeleton-based reasoning prompting is a promising training-free approach for structuring LLM reasoning, but prior work largely assumes an English-centric setting. We propose the Language-Aware Skeleton Exploration Framework (LASEF) to study skeleton-language choice in multilingual mathematical reasoning. Across math benchmarks, model scales, and languages, we show that English skeletons yield a small positive tendency on average, most visible for smaller models and low-resource languages. However, few language-level gains remain significant after correction, and English is not universally optimal. Combining greedy decoding, multi-rollout evaluation, translation ablation, and cross-benchmark validation, we further find three patterns of skeleton-language effects: directionally consistent, evaluation- and benchmark-dependent, and asymmetric negative. These effects cannot be fully explained by generation quality alone. Overall, skeleton language is a context-dependent design variable that requires multi-level exploration. All resources are released at https://github.com/lhsstn/LASEF.

</details>


### [188] [On-Policy Distillation Teaches New Skills but Not New Knowledge](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.09639)
*Yixuan Tang,Yi Yang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文通过受控合成框架与真实任务实验，证明反向KL在线策略蒸馏（OPD）能迁移组合技能而非事实知识，其知识迁移能力受KL方向与rollout设计支配。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在线策略蒸馏（OPD）已被证明能增强语言模型推理能力，但学生模型究竟获得了新的参数化事实知识，还是仅学到多步推理的组合技能，此前尚不清楚；这一区分对理解蒸馏机制与设计训练配方至关重要。

Method: 采用受控合成框架，先测量学生模型的初始能力，再独立控制教师额外注入的事实、组合技能或两者，从而解耦两类能力；在来自3个模型家族的4个模型上比较反向KL与正向KL、以及是否使用学生rollout的蒸馏配方消融；并在近期事实问答与竞赛数学任务上验证。

Result: 反向KL OPD能可靠迁移组合技能到未见推理结构，但事实知识迁移极少；将反向KL替换为正向KL可恢复事实迁移，而学生rollout专门提升多步推理的执行能力；真实任务上呈同样不对称性：推理收益显著而参数化知识未扩张。

Conclusion: 在线策略蒸馏并不扩展模型的参数化知识，而是教会模型组织与组合其已掌握的知识；知识迁移与技能迁移可由KL方向与rollout设计分别调控。

Abstract: On-policy distillation (OPD) strengthens language-model reasoning, yet whether students acquire new factual knowledge or compositional skill for multi-step reasoning remains unknown. We separate these capabilities using a controlled synthetic framework that measures the student's initial capabilities and independently controls the teacher's additional facts, compositional skill, or both. Across four models from three families, reverse-KL OPD reliably transfers compositional skill across unseen reasoning structures, but transfers minimal factual knowledge. Decoupling the distillation recipe reveals the source of this asymmetry: replacing reverse KL with forward KL restores factual transfer, whereas student rollouts specifically improve the execution of multi-step reasoning. Experiments on recent factual QA and competition mathematics show a similar asymmetry under reverse-KL OPD, yielding notable reasoning gains without factual memory expansion. Together, these results demonstrate that on-policy distillation does not expand a model's parametric knowledge, but instead teaches it to organize and compose the knowledge it already possesses.

</details>


### [189] [Rubric Spans are Label Representations: Joint LLM Encoding for Short Answer Scoring](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.09660)
*Zhifan Sun,Sebastian Gombert,Fabian Zehner,Leon Camus,Longwei Cong,Hendrik Drachsler*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出RUSPAN，一种以评分标准描述（rubric）作为语义标签表征的条件化ASAS框架，单次LM前向即完成列表式打分；其RIM变体通过阻止rubric片段互相注意，提升零样本迁移能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自动短答案评分（ASAS）需要模型能够依据针对具体题目的评分标准为学生作答打分，同时兼顾效率与跨评分标准集合的可迁移性。现有方法在评分标准变化或跨语言/跨结构迁移时表现受限。

Method: RUSPAN将题目上下文、学生答案以及全部候选评分等级序列化为单一序列，在一次语言模型前向中，基于rubric片段表征与整序列表征对所有等级进行列表式打分。进一步提出RUSPAN-RIM，引入Rubric-Independent Mask（RIM），阻止各rubric片段之间的相互注意，使评分标准表征仅依赖答案与题目上下文，避免训练中过拟合于rubric模式，从而支持零样本迁移；并结合位置重索引（position reindexing）。

Result: 在涵盖英语、德语、葡萄牙语的六个ASAS基准上，RUSPAN在单基准评分任务上优于判别式与生成式基线；在PT-ASAG这一语言与评分标准结构双重偏移最强的留出基准上，配合位置重索引的RIM带来一致且显著的性能提升。

Conclusion: 将评分标准描述作为语义标签表征并结合列表式单次前向打分，可有效提升ASAS的效率与准确率；通过RIM解耦评分标准片段间的注意力依赖，能显著增强跨语言与跨评分标准结构的零样本迁移能力。

Abstract: Automatic Short Answer Scoring (ASAS) requires models that can score student responses against question-specific criteria while remaining efficient and transferable across rubric sets. We propose RUSPAN, a rubric-conditioned ASAS framework that treats rubric descriptions as semantic label representations. RUSPAN serialises the question context, student answer, and all candidate rubric levels into a single sequence, then scores the levels listwise from the rubric-span and whole-sequence representations produced in a single LM pass. We further introduce RUSPAN-RIM, in which a Rubric-Independent Mask prevents rubric spans from attending to one another, making rubric representations depend only on the answer and question context and preventing overfitting to rubric patterns during training for zero-shot transfer. On six ASAS benchmarks spanning English, German, and Portuguese, RUSPAN improves mono-benchmark scoring over discriminative and generative baselines, while RIM with position reindexing delivers consistent and substantial gains on PT-ASAG, the held-out benchmark with the strongest combined language and rubric-structure shift.

</details>


### [190] [Alice: A Large-Scale German Benchmark for Rubric-Based Multi-Dimensional Automatic Short Answer Scoring](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.09661)
*Zhifan Sun,Sebastian Gombert,Jannik Lossjew,Tobias Wyrwich,Berrit Katharina Czinczel,David Bednorz,Marcus Kubsch,Knut Neumann,Hendrik Drachsler*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文介绍了 Alice，一个大规模、基于评分标准的德语自动短答案评分（ASAS）数据集，包含学习表现、知识要素和技能三个子任务，并将基于评分标准的 ASAS 形式化为评分标准检索任务，对多种语言模型进行了基准评测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有自动短答案评分（ASAS）的公开基准稀缺，且大多关注学生直接回答问题的好坏，而非其对底层概念（知识要素）和技能（如推理、论证）的掌握程度。为填补这一空白，作者构建了新的数据集。

Method: 作者提出 Alice——一个大规模、基于评分标准、教学对齐的德语 ASAS 数据集，包含三个子任务：学习表现（Alice-LP）、知识要素（Alice-KE）和技能（Alice-SK）。同时将基于评分标准的 ASAS 形式化为评分标准检索任务，并使用从 encoder-only 模型到轻量级 LLM 的一系列语言模型进行基准测试，还采用 LLM 零样本提示和标准分类基线进行评测。

Result: 实验显示，LLM 在零样本设置下对知识要素和技能的评分尤为困难。评分标准文本通常很有用，尤其对 Alice-KE 和 Alice-SK；而在 Alice-LP 上，相比聚焦样本解答的输入，收益较为有限，且因模型和输入格式而异。

Conclusion: Alice 为基于评分标准的德语 ASAS 提供了大规模、教学对齐的基准，将任务重新定义为评分标准检索，并揭示了当前语言模型（尤其零样本 LLM）在评估知识要素和技能方面的局限，同时表明评分标准文本对提升评分有重要作用。

Abstract: Automatic Short Answer Scoring (ASAS) is central to NLP for Education. However, openly available benchmarks remain scarce, and existing datasets largely address how well students answer a question directly rather than how well they master underlying concepts (knowledge elements) such as thermal energy or epistemic activities (skills) such as reasoning or claim.
  To address this gap, we introduce Alice, a large-scale, rubric-based German ASAS dataset that is pedagogically aligned and comprises three subtasks: (i) learning performance (Alice-LP), (ii) knowledge elements (Alice-KE), and (iii) skills (Alice-SK).
  We further formulate rubric-based ASAS as a rubric-retrieval task and benchmark the dataset with a range of language models, from encoder-only models to lightweight LLMs. We also benchmark the dataset with zero-shot prompting via LLMs and a standard classification baseline. The experiments show that LLMs, in particular, struggle to score knowledge elements and skills in the zero-shot setting. They also indicate that rubric text is often useful, especially for Alice-KE and Alice-SK, while on Alice-LP gains over sample-solution-focused inputs are more modest and vary by model and input format.

</details>


### [191] [SAPD: Step-Aligned Privileged Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.09665)
*Tianle Wang,Jiayu Liu,Ruizhi Zhao,Ning Miao*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出SAPD，一种无需rollout的自蒸馏方法，将演示转化为步对齐的分布级监督，在数学推理上优于SFT与标签平滑，可比拟on-policy RL，且训练循环提速约2倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: on-policy后训练虽能利用模型自身轨迹提升大模型，但rollout生成代价高昂。作者探究固定演示能否通过更好的监督支持有竞争力的off-policy学习，核心假设是演示的价值不仅取决于训练轨迹本身，还取决于监督能否在候选续写间提供有信息量的偏好，并将这种指导与当前所学推理决策相连接。

Method: 提出Step-Aligned Privileged Distillation（SAPD），一种无rollout的自蒸馏方法：利用参考解的已知推进过程，将每步推理转移与有针对性的特权指导相关联，从而把演示转化为步对齐的分布级监督，而非将解视为无差别的上下文。

Result: 在数学推理基准上，SAPD平均优于监督微调与标签平滑，同时与on-policy强化学习和自蒸馏相当；消融分析支持了上下文相关分布指导的价值以及将特权信息与当前步对齐的收益；SAPD基本保持了域外代码性能，并在训练循环上相较on-policy基线取得约2倍加速。

Conclusion: 精心构造的监督可使完全off-policy的后训练成为有竞争力且计算高效的替代方案。

Abstract: On-policy post-training can improve large language models by learning from their own trajectories, but requires costly rollout generation. We ask whether fixed demonstrations can support competitive off-policy learning through better supervision. Our premise is that their usefulness depends not only on the training trajectories, but also on whether supervision provides informative preferences among continuations and connects this guidance to the reasoning decision being learned. We introduce Step-Aligned Privileged Distillation (SAPD), a rollout-free self-distillation method that turns demonstrations into step-aligned distributional supervision. Its key insight is to use the known progression of a reference solution to associate each reasoning transition with targeted privileged guidance, rather than treating the solution as undifferentiated context. On mathematical reasoning benchmarks, SAPD outperforms supervised fine-tuning and label smoothing on average while remaining competitive with on-policy reinforcement learning and self-distillation. Analyses support both the value of context-dependent distributional guidance and the benefit of aligning privileged information with the current step. SAPD also largely preserves out-of-domain coding performance and achieves approximately 2x training-loop speedups over the on-policy baselines. These findings suggest that carefully constructed supervision can make fully off-policy post-training a competitive and computationally efficient alternative. Our code is available at https://github.com/Miaow-Lab/SAPD.

</details>


### [192] [InsClaimBench: Benchmarking Insurance Claim Adjudication Across the Decision Chain](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.09671)
*Linqi Zhang,Chong Qi,Yan Cheng,Wanqing Cao,Yu Liu,Chenwei Lin,Xian Xu*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出 InsClaimBench，一个面向保险理赔裁决全决策链的端到端基准，包含 3,780 个案件、375 个案件族与 86,656 条原子规则判断，评测六个 LLM 后发现决策链上可靠性逐级衰减。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 推理型大模型发展促使人们评估其专业决策能力，而保险理赔裁决需要模型串联案件证据、保险规则、中间判断与赔付计算，现有缺乏覆盖完整决策链的评测基准。

Method: 基于真实理赔材料与结构化保险规则构建 InsClaimBench，覆盖车险、财产险与健康险，含 3,780 个案件、375 个案件族、86,656 条原子规则判断；从原子规则到裁决模块再到赔付决策与金额逐层评测，并设计受控事实变体检验变更能否在各级正确传播；对六个 LLM 进行评估。

Result: 赔付决策准确率为 74.23%–80.19%，而决策—金额联合准确率降至 47.54%–73.15%；原子规则准确率高达 95.48%，但规则向量完全匹配最高仅 36.90%；最高频模块错误未必与最终决策失败最相关；事实变更下模块更新比规则更新更不可靠，正确局部判断仍可能产生错误赔付，正确赔付也可能掩盖中间错误。

Conclusion: 可靠的理赔裁决要求决策链上具备一致性的组合与传播能力，局部高准确率不足以保障案件级正确性。

Abstract: Recent advances in reasoning-oriented large language models (LLMs) have motivated increasing evaluation of their ability to perform professional decision tasks. Insurance claim adjudication is one such task, requiring models to connect case evidence, insurance rules, intermediate judgments, and payout calculations across a structured decision process. We introduce InsClaimBench, an end-to-end benchmark for evaluating insurance claim adjudication across the decision chain. Grounded in real claim materials and structured insurance rules, InsClaimBench contains 3,780 cases in 375 case families across auto, property, and health insurance, comprising 86,656 atomic rule judgments. It evaluates each claim from atomic rules through adjudication modules to payout decisions and amounts, with controlled factual variants testing whether required changes are correctly propagated across levels. Evaluation of six LLMs reveals a progressive loss of reliability along the decision chain. Payout-decision accuracy ranges from 74.23--80.19%, while joint decision--amount accuracy drops to 47.54--73.15%. Strong local performance also fails to ensure case-level correctness: atomic-rule accuracy reaches 95.48%, whereas rule-vector exact match peaks at only 36.90%, and the most frequent module errors are not necessarily those most associated with final-decision failure. Under factual changes, these inconsistencies further become propagation failures: module updates are less reliable than rule updates, correct local judgments can still yield incorrect payouts, and correct payouts can conceal intermediate errors. These results show that reliable claim adjudication requires consistent composition and propagation across the decision chain.

</details>


### [193] [From Pareto to Preference: Personalized Test-Time Scaling via Amortized Agentic Policy Discovery](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.09684)
*Xinglin Wang,Zishen Liu,Tong Zheng,Shaoxiong Feng,Peiwen Yuan,Yiwei Li,Jiayi Shi,Yueqi Zhang,Chuyi Tan,Ji Zhang,Boyuan Pan,Kan Li*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出 PersonTTS，将个性化测试时缩放（TTS）形式化为求解最大化用户多维需求联合满足率的可执行控制器，并用摊销式智能体策略发现框架复用既往搜索经验，在 AIME/HMMT 上显著优于强基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 TTS 效率优化方法通常一次只针对单一资源维度，分别推进"精度—成本"或"精度—延迟"帕累托前沿；但用户需求是多维的，可能同时指定精度、延迟与推理成本，且不同需求组合偏好不同控制器，因此需要面向用户个性化需求联合优化的策略发现方法。

Method: 首先将个性化测试时缩放形式化为发现可执行控制器、以最大化用户特定需求的联合满足率。为降低面向新用户画像重复做策略发现的开销，提出 PersonTTS——一种摊销式智能体策略发现框架：通过需求匹配的控制器初始化与源蒸馏的过程性指导来复用既往搜索经验，同时对每个候选策略保留目标画像评估。

Result: 在 AIME 与 HMMT 上，PersonTTS 在未见用户画像与留出问题上的联合需求满足率显著优于强 TTS 基线；在相同候选评估预算下，跨用户经验复用进一步提升策略质量，并大幅减少发现智能体的时间与成本。

Conclusion: 个性化 TTS 可作为多维需求的联合优化问题，通过摊销式、经验复用的智能体策略发现高效求解；经验复用在不增加评估预算的前提下兼顾策略质量与发现效率。

Abstract: Test-time scaling (TTS) improves the reasoning capabilities of large language models by allocating additional inference computation. Existing approaches to improving TTS efficiency largely optimize accuracy against one resource dimension at a time, advancing either the accuracy--cost or accuracy--latency Pareto frontier. Yet user requirements are multidimensional: users may specify accuracy, latency, and inference-cost requirements jointly, and different requirements can favor different controllers. We formulate Personalized Test-Time Scaling as discovering executable controllers that maximize the joint satisfaction rate of user-specific requirements. To reduce the overhead of repeated policy discovery for new user profiles, we propose PersonTTS, an amortized agentic policy-discovery framework that reuses prior search experience through requirement-matched controller initialization and source-distilled procedural guidance, while retaining target-profile evaluation for every candidate. Experiments on AIME and HMMT show that PersonTTS substantially outperforms strong TTS baselines in joint requirement satisfaction on unseen user profiles and held-out problems. Under the same candidate-evaluation budget, cross-user experience reuse further improves policy quality while substantially reducing discovery-agent time and cost.

</details>


### [194] [SpikingVLA: Asynchronous Spiking Vision-Language-Action Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.09710)
*Jingya Wang,Dehao Zhang,Shuai Wang,Malu Zhang,Yang Yang,Haizhou Li*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: SpikingVLA是一个ANN到SNN的转换框架，通过DIF神经元与异步执行机制，实现低延迟高精度的脉冲VLA推理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: ANN到SNN转换可避免从头训练大规模SNN的高成本，是构建高能效脉冲VLA模型的实用途径。但现有方法需大量时间步才能保持竞争力，导致实时VLA部署的推理延迟过高。

Method: 提出Dendritic Integrate-and-Fire (DIF)神经元，通过树突混合与自适应胞体发放缓解通道级激活离群值，以更少时间步实现精确转换；基于DIF神经元引入异步执行机制，重叠VLA各组件的时序计算，降低同步开销与延迟。

Result: SpikingVLA在导航任务上取得有竞争力的性能与显著提升的推理效率。相比现有脉冲VLA方法，SR与SPL分别提升11.9%和12.6%，首个动作延迟降低11.2倍。

Conclusion: SpikingVLA可作为部署预训练VLA模型、实现高性能低延迟脉冲推理的实用框架。

Abstract: ANN-to-SNN conversion offers a practical route toward energy-efficient spiking Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models by bypassing the substantial cost of training large-scale SNNs from scratch. However, existing methods often require many timesteps to maintain competitive performance, resulting in substantial inference latency for real-time VLA deployment. To address this challenge, we introduce SpikingVLA, an ANN-to-SNN conversion framework that enables accurate and low-latency spiking VLA inference. Specifically, we propose a Dendritic Integrate-and-Fire (DIF) neuron that alleviates channel-wise activation outliers through dendritic mixing and adaptive somatic firing, enabling accurate ANN-to-SNN conversion with fewer timesteps. Building on DIF neurons, we further introduce an asynchronous execution mechanism that overlaps temporal computation across VLA components, reducing synchronization overhead and latency. Extensive experiments demonstrate that SpikingVLA achieves competitive navigation performance with substantially improved inference efficiency. Compared with existing spiking VLA methods, SpikingVLA improves SR and SPL by 11.9\% and 12.6\%, respectively, while reducing first-action latency by 11.2$\times$. These results establish SpikingVLA as a practical framework for deploying pretrained VLA models with high-performance and low-latency spiking inference.

</details>


### [195] [Bridge Routing Heads: Where Multilingual Multi-hop Reasoning Lives in LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.09733)
*Seunghan Kim,Minyeong Choe,Hyunil Kim,Haehyun Cho*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该研究揭示多语言大模型进行多跳推理时使用"语言特异"的内部回路，通过桥接路由头（BRH）的激活级干预可在无训练条件下恢复跨语言推理失败。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多语言LLM对同一多跳问题在不同语言下作答，但其内部是否共享同一回路缺乏机制性解释。作者旨在识别跨语言推理所依赖的内部电路，并检验其是否为语言共享还是语言特异。

Method: 提出三阶段流水线，在Llama 3.1 70B与Qwen 2.5 72B两个大型多语言LLM中识别桥接路由头（Bridge Routing Heads, BRH）。通过头集间的Jaccard相似度衡量跨语言重叠；用消融（ablation）与对失败目标语言通道的放大（amplification）进行因果验证。

Result: 语言特异头集在五种语言间近乎完全互斥，Llama 3.1 70B平均Jaccard仅0.017，Qwen 2.5 72B为0.057。消融通用BRH使两跳负对数似然（NLL）上升为随机头基线的39–89倍，提供直接因果证据。放大这些头可无训练地挽救最高51.7%的跨语言失败。两模型共享"双回路"模式但分配不同：Llama将链式推理集中于一个大型通用池，Qwen更依赖较大的语言特异池。

Conclusion: 多语言LLM的跨语言多跳推理依赖语言特异回路而非共享回路；仅靠激活级干预即可从跨语言推理失败中恢复正确答案。

Abstract: Multilingual LLMs answer the same multi-hop reasoning question across languages, but we lack a mechanistic account of whether they share an internal circuit. We identify Bridge Routing Heads (BRH) in two large multilingual LLMs through a three-stage pipeline. The resulting language-specific head sets exhibit near-complete mutual exclusivity across the five languages, with a mean Jaccard similarity of only 0.017 for Llama 3.1 70B and 0.057 for Qwen 2.5 72B, revealing language-idiosyncratic circuits. Ablating general BRH increases two-hop Negative Log-Likelihood (NLL) by 39-89x the random-head baseline, providing direct causal evidence of their role. Amplifying these heads in a failing target-language pass rescues up to 51.7% of cross-lingual failures, with no training. The two models share this dual-circuit pattern but allocate heads differently: Llama concentrates chaining in a large general pool, while Qwen leans on larger language-specific pools. Together these results show that activation-level intervention alone can recover correct answers from cross-lingual reasoning failures.

</details>


### [196] [Shaer: Controlled Arabic Poetry Generation with Meter Subform and Semantic Conditioning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.09756)
*Ahmad Abbas,Tamara Fakih,Nour Fakih,Ammar Mohanna*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出 Shaer，一个可控的古典阿拉伯语诗歌生成框架，同时以自然语言描述、韵律子形式（meter subform）和半句数（hemistich counts）为条件，并构建了含 116,032 首诗的增强语料库，基于 Yehia-7B 用 QLoRA 进行监督微调。系统在基础韵律准确率、诗歌级子形式准确率和精确长度准确率上分别达到 95.17%、91.75% 和 83.40%，且未发现对训练语料的复制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 古典阿拉伯语诗歌生成需同时满足语义、语言和细粒度韵律约束。现有系统通常只能控制宽泛的诗歌属性，无法联合建模语义意图、韵律子形式和诗歌长度，因此需要一个新的可控生成框架。

Method: 1）构建增强语料库：从 Ashaar 整理 116,032 首古典阿拉伯语诗歌，包含归一化的韵律子形式标签和自动生成并验证的语义描述；2）模型适配：基于 Yehia-7B，采用 QLoRA 监督微调，使用仅补全（completion-only）目标；3）评估设计：结合基础韵律一致性、请求子形式遵循度和长度控制的自动评估，并引入三个 LLM 评判、盲测人工评估和记忆化分析。

Result: Shaer 实现基础韵律准确率 95.17%、诗歌级韵律子形式准确率 91.75%、精确半句数准确率 83.40%。相较未微调的基础模型，三项指标分别提升 68.68、57.77 和 38.93 个百分点；在所有评估系统中基础韵律准确率最高。多 LLM 评估与对最优输出的盲测人工评估表明其语义与文学质量具有竞争力。对全部 3,481 条测试生成结果的分析未发现与训练语料或配对源诗的完全复制。

Conclusion: Shaer 能够联合控制语义、韵律子形式和长度，在韵律合规性与质量上显著优于基线，并具备较强的抗记忆化能力。代码、模型与数据集已公开。

Abstract: Classical Arabic poetry generation requires simultaneously satisfying semantic, linguistic, and fine-grained prosodic constraints. Existing systems typically control broad poetic attributes but do not jointly model semantic intent, meter subform, and poem length. We present Shaer, a controllable Classical Arabic poetry generation framework jointly conditioned on natural-language descriptions, meter subforms, and target hemistich counts. To support this task, we construct an enriched corpus of 116,032 classical Arabic poems derived from Ashaar, containing normalized meter-subform labels and automatically generated, validated semantic descriptions. We then adapt Yehia-7B using QLoRA-based supervised fine-tuning with a completion-only objective. Our evaluation combines automatic assessment of base-meter conformity, requested-subform adherence, and length control with three LLM judges, blinded human evaluation, and memorization analysis. Shaer achieves 95.17% base-meter accuracy, 91.75% poem-level meter-subform accuracy, and 83.40% exact count accuracy. Relative to its untuned foundation model, these results represent gains of 68.68, 57.77, and 38.93 percentage points, respectively; Shaer also attains the highest base-meter accuracy among all evaluated systems. Multi-LLM evaluation and a blinded human assessment of top-ranked outputs further indicate competitive semantic and literary quality. Finally, analysis of all 3,481 test generations finds no exact copies from the training corpus or paired source poems. Code, models, and datasets are publicly available.

</details>


### [197] [Decoupling Logic from Persona: Structural Immunity of Edge LLM Agents to Context Pollution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.09772)
*Masaaki Nakatsu,Reno Wang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该论文研究边缘设备上小语言模型智能体在长对话历史与人格指令混杂时出现的"人格-逻辑干扰"问题，提出解耦架构AO-DA，将逻辑推理（"What"）与人格表达（"How"）拆分为同一INT4基座模型上的两条推理路径，用可热插拔LoRA适配器分别承载。实验表明解耦逻辑路径对污染完全不变，而单通道混合路径性能单调退化；解耦在8B模型上更鲁棒，但在4B模型上两者均崩溃。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 边缘设备上的小语言模型智能体需在同一上下文窗口内同时维持人格设定并正确推理，但对话历史与人格指令会不断挤占上下文。论文关注长时间、误导性且人格密集的历史如何损害智能体的逻辑推理能力，即"人格-逻辑干扰"（persona-logic interference）问题。

Method: 提出解耦架构AO-DA：在单一INT4基座模型上通过可热插拔LoRA适配器将推理分为两条路径。逻辑路径仅接收核心轮次，输出可验证的结构化状态（Micro-State）；人格路径结合完整历史将其渲染为人格化表达。在Apple M2笔记本上以Llama-3.1-8B-Instruct与Gemma-3-4B-it（4-bit）进行同基座消融，共480次运行（4个污染等级×3个臂×2任务×2人格×5种子）。

Result: (i) 解耦逻辑路径对污染具结构不变性：提示词长度固定为180（Llama）/167（Gemma）token，而混合单通道提示词从242增至1,203，输出在各污染等级间逐字节一致（40/40）。(ii) 混合单通道单调退化：Llama综合逻辑分从0.669降至0.150，Gemma从0.487降至0.150，主要因未能输出所需结构化结果（Llama 80-95%，Gemma在两个最高等级100%）。(iii) 将相同污染输入解耦逻辑路径，配备专用适配器与格式的路径在8B模型上仍更鲁棒（失败率0-20% vs 80-95%；配对Δ +0.30至+0.50，Cliff's δ 0.50-0.85，Holm校正p≤0.03），但在4B模型上两者均崩溃。分离代价为话题首轮多一次解码（Llama 28.2s vs 18.2s），换来1.7ms的人格热切换且无需重跑逻辑路径。

Conclusion: 将逻辑推理与人格表达解耦可有效抵御长历史与人格指令带来的污染，使逻辑路径保持结构与输出稳定，并以极小延迟代价实现人格热切换；该方案在8B模型上显著优于单通道混合推理，但受基座能力限制，在4B模型上优势消失。代码、评分标准、测试样例、适配器与日志均已开源。

Abstract: Small language-model agents on edge devices must hold a persona and reason correctly at once, inside one context window that fills with conversational history and persona instructions. We study what happens to the logical part of such an agent when that history is long, misleading and persona-heavy (persona-logic interference), and present a Decoupling Architecture (AO-DA) that separates logical inference ("What") from persona expression ("How") into two inference paths on one INT4 base model with hot-swappable LoRA adapters. The logic path receives only the core turn and emits a verifiable structured state (Micro-State); the persona path renders it in character with the full history. In same-base-model ablations on an Apple M2 laptop (Llama-3.1-8B-Instruct and Gemma-3-4B-it, 4-bit; 480 runs over 4 pollution levels x 3 arms x 2 tasks x 2 personas x 5 seeds) we find: (i) the decoupled logic path is structurally invariant to pollution: its prompt stays at 180 (Llama) or 167 (Gemma) tokens while the mixed single-pass prompt grows from 242 to 1,203, and its outputs are byte-identical across levels (40/40); (ii) the mixed single pass degrades monotonically (composite logic score 0.669 to 0.150 on Llama, 0.487 to 0.150 on Gemma), mostly by failing to emit the required structured output (80-95% of runs on Llama, 100% on Gemma at the two highest levels); (iii) with the same pollution fed into the decoupled logic path, the dedicated-adapter, dedicated-format path is still more robust than the single pass on the 8B model (failure 0-20% vs 80-95%; paired $Δ$ +0.30 to +0.50, Cliff's $δ$ 0.50-0.85, Holm-adjusted $p \le 0.03$) but not on the 4B model, where both collapse. Separation costs one extra decode on a topic's first turn (28.2 s vs 18.2 s on Llama) and buys persona hot-swapping in 1.7 ms without re-running the logic path. Code, rubric, fixtures, adapters and logs are released.

</details>


### [198] [Judging in Latent Space: Efficient Generative Reward Modeling via Semantics-Preserving Compression](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.09788)
*Mingqing Yuan,Xiaobo Liang,Junwei Yang,Ziwei Chen,Zeren Zhang,Hejin Wang,Yubin Wang,Juntao Li*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: LatentGRM：基于语义分块-压缩-重构的潜在评估框架，用紧凑连续轨迹替代逐token文本评判，在4B/8B规模下以8.9–9.2倍轨迹压缩和6.1–7.0倍推理加速达到与显式SFT评判器相当的偏好准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 奖励建模通常需在多条评估标准上联合表示与推理，而逐token地将这一过程语言化会带来显著的推理开销。近期潜在推理研究提示连续状态可能更紧凑地承载此类计算，因此作者希望在不牺牲评判质量的前提下大幅降低评判推理成本。

Method: 提出LatentGRM，一个基于语义分块（semantic chunking）、压缩（compression）与重构（reconstruction）的潜在评估框架。利用评分准则引导评估的结构来指导压缩，学习紧凑的连续轨迹，从而在不再生成文本评估的情况下自主完成成对判断；另设独立解释器（interpreter）从这些轨迹重构评估文本，用于离线考察压缩后保留的信息。实验在匹配训练数据与骨干网络的条件下，于4B和8B规模与显式SFT评判器对比，并实施受控的评分准则干预实验。

Result: 在4B与8B规模上，LatentGRM取得与显式SFT评判器相当的总体偏好准确率。在四个基准领域上，LatentGRM-8B将评估轨迹压缩至8.9–9.2倍，并在vote@5下将评判器总推理时间降低6.1–7.0倍。受控评分准则干预表明，依赖具体标准的偏好信息确实通过潜在序列传递。

Conclusion: 连续潜在评估能够在保持有竞争力的判断质量的同时显著降低推理开销，验证了以潜在轨迹替代显式文本评判的可行性。

Abstract: Reward modeling often requires jointly representing and reasoning over multiple evaluation criteria, yet verbalizing this process token by token can incur substantial inference cost. Recent work on latent reasoning suggests that continuous states may support this computation more compactly. We introduce LatentGRM, a latent evaluation framework built on semantic chunking, compression, and reconstruction. By using the structure of rubric-guided evaluations to guide compression, LatentGRM learns compact continuous trajectories that support autonomous pairwise judgments without generating textual assessments. A separate interpreter reconstructs evaluation text from these trajectories, providing an offline view of the information retained under compression. Under matched training data and backbones, LatentGRM achieves competitive aggregate preference accuracy relative to explicit Supervised Fine-Tuning (SFT) judges at both 4B and 8B scales. Across four benchmark domains, LatentGRM-8B compresses evaluation trajectories by 8.9--9.2x and reduces total judge inference time by 6.1--7.0x at vote@5. Controlled rubric interventions show that criterion-dependent preference information is carried through the latent sequence. Together, these results demonstrate that continuous latent evaluation can substantially reduce inference cost while preserving competitive judgment quality.

</details>


### [199] [MIRROR: From Imitation to Internalization in LLM Personalization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.09795)
*Huayi Lai,Jicheng Yang,Min Yi,Chong Meng*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出自蒸馏框架MIRROR及其插件MIRROR-F，将LLM个性化从参考文本模仿转向用户偏好内化，在多个个性化生成基准上取得领先的整体个性化表现与更优文本质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 个性化LLM的需求正从风格模仿转向内容质量。现有微调范式难以弥合这一差距，作者探究自蒸馏能否桥接该缺口，并针对现有范式的局限提出新方法。

Method: MIRROR（Meta-personalization by Internalizing Reference-Revealed On-policy Reflections）框架：用"参考揭示的在线自蒸馏"替代参考token模仿，使模型在自身生成轨迹上将下一token分布与其"参考条件化的自身"对齐，从而内化用户偏好而非复现参考措辞。MIRROR-F为聚焦式插件，在在线分布对齐基础上对信息量高的参考token施加选择性监督，在保持用户特定表达的同时强化内容生成。

Result: 在三个个性化生成基准、两种模型规模，以及基于参考与基于LLM的互补评估下，MIRROR和MIRROR-F取得领先的整体个性化性能和更优文本质量；在三个未见过的个性化生成任务上，相比基于SFT的基线出现更少的灾难性遗忘。增益在不同模型规模和应用场景中保持一致。

Conclusion: 自蒸馏可有效桥接个性化LLM从风格模仿到内容质量的转变；通过内化用户偏好而非复制参考措辞，MIRROR与MIRROR-F在提升个性化与文本质量的同时缓解灾难性遗忘，并具备跨规模、跨场景的稳定性，可改善LLM个性化任务表现。

Abstract: The demand for personalized LLMs is shifting from style imitation toward content quality. We investigate whether self-distillation can bridge this gap in existing fine-tuning paradigm. To address this limitation, we introduce MIRROR(Meta- personalization by Internalizing Reference-Revealed On-policy Reflections), a novel self-distillation framework that shifts LLM personalization from imitation toward preference internalization. First, we replace reference-token imitation with reference-revealed on-policy self-distillation, aligning the model's next-token distributions along its own generation trajectories with those of its reference-conditioned self, thereby internalizing user preferences rather than reproducing reference wording.Second, we introduce MIRROR-F, a focal plug-in that augments on-policy distributional alignment with selective supervision over informative reference tokens, thereby strengthening content generation while preserving user-specific expression. Across three personalized generation benchmarks, two model scales, and complementary reference-based and LLM-based evaluations, MIRROR and MIRROR-F achieve leading overall personalization performance and superior text quality, while exhibiting less catastrophic forgetting than SFT-based baselines on three unseen personalized generation tasks. The gains are consistent across model scales and application scenarios, translating to improved performance in LLM personalization tasks.

</details>


### [200] [A Deafening Silence: Catastrophic Forgetting Lives in the Output Embeddings of Tokens the Data Never Speaks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.09835)
*Jonghyun Han,Younghoon Song,Jongyoul Park*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该论文在无原始数据的持续预训练/微调场景下定位了灾难性遗忘的发生位置与机制，并提出仅提升输出投影层Adam的epsilon即可显著缓解遗忘。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型的持续预训练与微调会不可避免地引发灾难性遗忘，常规缓解方法依赖回放（replay）原始数据，但原始数据往往无法获取。论文希望在无数据（data-free）条件下理解遗忘发生在哪里、为何发生，并找到有效的干预手段。

Method: 1) 在最多1.4B参数的模型上系统性地冻结不同参数，覆盖五种设置，定位遗忘的集中位置；2) 分析遗忘与语料词表匮乏（vocabulary deficiency）的关系及梯度机制；3) 提出在训练中仅对输出投影层提高Adam的epsilon进行干预；4) 在160M至12B参数、四个模型家族的八种设置上验证，并测试与replay及LoRA的兼容性，同时检验事后编辑漂移行（post-hoc editing）的效果。

Result: 遗忘选择性地集中在new corpus中少见token的输出embedding上，而body中同一sqrt(v-hat)波段保持惰性、新知识学习发生在别处；该定位由语料的词表匮乏决定而非训练模式，故可在固定基座模型下仅凭token计数进行重训练前的风险排序。机制上，缺失token会持续获得单侧softmax梯度，被Adam二阶矩（sqrt(v-hat)）归一化放大为全尺寸更新。所提干预在八种设置下消除了39.4%至67.9%的遗忘（七个稳定配置），且不损害目标学习、无需逐模型调参；与replay可叠加或更优（Qwen/韩语上达79.8%），并挽救发布头（released-head）LoRA使其免于23倍遗忘激增。

Conclusion: 由于对漂移行进行事后编辑只能恢复不到5%的遗忘，干预必须在训练过程中进行。研究表明，当语料使词表"饥饿"时，单行优化器调整（提高输出投影层的Adam epsilon）可作为对抗灾难性遗忘的主要防御手段。

Abstract: Continual pre-training and fine-tuning in Large Language Models (LLMs) inevitably induce catastrophic forgetting, typically mitigated by replay using often-inaccessible original data. In this data-free regime, we analyze where forgetting occurs and why. Systematic parameter freezing across five settings up to 1.4B reveals that forgetting concentrates selectively in the output embeddings of tokens rarely seen in the new corpus, whereas the same sqrt(v-hat) band of the body is inert and new learning resides elsewhere. This localization is governed by the vocabulary deficiency of the corpus rather than the training mode, allowing pre-retraining risk ranking from token counts alone within a fixed base model. Mechanistically, absent tokens receive persistent one-sided softmax gradients that Adam's second-moment (sqrt(v-hat)) normalization amplifies into full-sized updates. We therefore propose an intervention: raising Adam's epsilon exclusively for the output projection during training. Across eight settings spanning 160M to 12B parameters and four model families, this removes 39.4% to 67.9% of forgetting across all seven stable configurations without degrading target learning or requiring per-model tuning. The defense combines additively or better with replay (79.8% on Qwen/Korean) and rescues released-head LoRA from a 23-fold forgetting surge. Because post-hoc editing of the drifted rows recovers under 5% of forgetting, the intervention must operate during training. Our findings indicate that a single-line optimizer adjustment may serve as the primary defense against catastrophic forgetting where the corpus starves the vocabulary.

</details>


### [201] [Training Advisors for LLM Agents from Task Outcomes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.09858)
*Sergei Polezhaev,Barys Liskavets,Ori Press,Alexander Golubev*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出 Caddie，一种用强化学习训练 critic 为 LLM 智能体提供自然语言分析与建议的方法，仅依赖任务最终成功与否作为奖励，训练的 critic 可跨不同基座模型与任务域迁移并显著提升成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 LLM 智能体在执行多步任务时交错进行推理与工具调用，虽已有工作表明自然语言反馈能帮助其修正决策，但如何训练 critic 生成有效反馈仍受限于对步级标注或参考评论（reference critiques）的依赖，这类监督信号昂贵且难以获取。

Method: Caddie 让 critic 在智能体执行任务过程中提供自然语言分析与建议；训练信号仅来自"智能体在收到 critic 反馈后是否最终成功"这一结果级奖励，而非步级标签或参考答案。采用强化学习优化 critic，同时冻结基座模型，并在多跳问答任务上以单一基座模型进行训练。

Result: 用 Qwen3-4B 基座训练的 critic 在四种不同规模与架构的基座模型上均提升成功率，其中三种未参与 critic 训练。在 MuSiQue 基准上，该 critic 使 Qwen3-4B 成功率提升超过 25 个百分点，超过无 critic 的 Kimi K3。同一 critic 在未额外训练的情况下，也在 τ³ 与 DeepDive 等域外交互式基准上带来增益。

Conclusion: 智能体能够在推理时自主决定何时向 critic 求助；基于结果（outcome-based）的 critic 训练可产生跨基座模型与跨任务域迁移的指导，说明无需步级标注或参考评论即可训练出通用性强的 critic。

Abstract: Large language model agents tackle multi-step tasks by interleaving reasoning and tool calls with observations from the environment. Prior work has shown that natural-language feedback can help these agents revise their decisions during task execution. We introduce Caddie, a method for training critics to provide natural-language analysis and advice as agents work through a task. Unlike approaches that rely on step-level labels or reference critiques, Caddie learns from whether the agent ultimately succeeds after receiving the critic's feedback. We optimize the critic through reinforcement learning while keeping the base model frozen. Trained on multi-hop question answering with a single base model, our Qwen3-4B critic improves success rates across four base models of different scales and architectures, including three not used during critic training. On the MuSiQue benchmark, the trained critic improves Qwen3-4B's success rate by more than 25 percentage points, surpassing the performance of Kimi K3 without a critic. The same critic also yields gains on out-of-domain interactive benchmarks, including $τ^3$ and DeepDive, with no additional training. Our results show that agents can decide when to seek help from a critic at inference time and that outcome-based critic training can produce guidance that transfers across base models and task domains.

</details>


### [202] [Itgan at NADI 2026 shared task: Parameter-Efficient Whisper Adaptation for Robust, Mixed-Dialect and Code-Switched Arabic ASR](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.09934)
*Ibrahim Almajai*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文介绍 Itgan 系统参加 NADI 2026 三个 ASR 子任务的方案：统一使用消费级 GPU 上 LoRA 微调的 Whisper 配方，并针对各子任务做不同改进。子任务1.1达到57.1%国家级平均WER；子任务1.2通过更换基座模型与去相关系统融合达到46.7% WER；子任务1.3以14.49% WER获第二名，CER为5.38%。所有比较均采用配对自助法检验，并报告了八种无效方向。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 面向 NADI 2026 的鲁棒国家级 ASR、混合方言 ASR 和突尼斯语码切换 ASR 三个子任务，探索在消费级 GPU 上用 LoRA 微调 Whisper 的统一高效方案，并以消融与系统融合提升性能。

Method: 三个子任务共享一套配方：在消费级 GPU 上用 LoRA 微调 Whisper。子任务1.1在提供方言标签时，基于池化适配器继续训练各方言专家模型；评估后用冻结编码器特征的线性探针实现无标签路由。子任务1.2比较基座模型与适配器容量，并采用引入去相关成员的系统融合。子任务1.3采用独立训练运行的精确权重空间平均，辅以 ROVER 投票。

Result: 子任务1.1提交系统达57.1%国家级平均WER，线性探针路由可恢复 oracle 路由增益的44%；子任务1.2达46.7% WER；子任务1.3以14.49% WER位列第二，CER为5.38%居领先提交最低。

Conclusion: 统一的 LoRA-Whisper 配方可有效支撑三个 NADI 2026 ASR 子任务；方言专家、去相关融合与权重平均等技术带来增益，且所有结论均有配对自助法检验支持。

Abstract: We describe the Itgan systems for the three ASR subtasks of NADI 2026, namely robust country-level ASR (1.1), mixed-dialect ASR (1.2), and Tunisian code-switched ASR (1.3). All three share one recipe, Whisper adapted with LoRA on consumer GPUs, and each was carried by a different addition to it. On 1.1, where the dialect label is given at test time, per-dialect specialists continued from a pooled adapter gave the largest gain, and the submitted system reached 57.1% country-average WER. A post-evaluation linear probe on frozen encoder features routes utterances without the label and recovers 44% of what oracle routing gives. On 1.2 the choice of base model mattered more than adapter capacity, and system combination helped only once we added a decorrelated member, reaching 46.7% WER. On 1.3 our system placed second at 14.49% WER with the lowest CER among the leading submissions, 5.38%. Its last 0.60 WER points came without further training, mostly from an exact weight-space average of independently trained runs, with ROVER voting adding the remainder. Every comparison carries a paired-bootstrap test, and we report eight directions that did not work.

</details>


### [203] [EASE: Entropy-Adaptive Distribution Shaping for Evading AI-generated Text Detectors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.09976)
*Jicheng Zhou,Kahim Wong,Jialong Wang,Jiantao Zhou*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出免训练、与检测器无关的规避框架EASE，通过基于预测熵自适应调整logit扰动与采样温度，降低AI生成文本检测性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI生成文本（AIGT）检测对源大语言模型（LLM）的解码选择敏感。作者观察到扰乱下一token的logit或调整采样温度即可降低检测性能，这揭示了检测器对解码阶段分布变化的脆弱性。基于此观察，作者希望利用这一脆弱性构造一种无需检测器反馈、无需微调的规避方法。

Method: EASE（Entropy-Adaptive Distribution Shaping for Evasion）：直接从源LLM的下一token分布计算预测熵，并用该熵自适应地同时调节logit扰动与采样温度。框架为免训练（training-free）且与检测器无关（detector-agnostic），不需要检测器反馈，也不需要模型微调。

Result: 在三个源LLM和多种检测器上的实验显示检测性能持续下降；同时文本质量退化可忽略，推理开销也可忽略。

Conclusion: 解码阶段的分布塑形（logit扰动与温度调节）是一种有效且低成本的AIGT检测规避途径；EASE以熵为自适应信号，实现了跨模型、跨检测器的一致性规避效果，且几乎不损害文本质量与效率。

Abstract: AI-generated text (AIGT) detection can be sensitive to the decoding choices of the source large language model (LLM). We observe that perturbing next-token logits or adjusting sampling temperature can reduce detection performance, providing a clear signal of detector vulnerability to decoding-time distribution changes. Building on this observation, we propose EASE (Entropy-Adaptive Distribution Shaping for Evasion), a training-free and detector-agnostic framework for evading AIGT detectors. EASE computes predictive entropy directly from the source LLM's next-token distribution and uses it to adapt both logit perturbation and sampling temperature, without detector feedback or model fine-tuning. Experiments across three source LLMs and multiple detectors demonstrate consistent reductions in detection performance, with negligible degradation in text quality and negligible inference overhead.

</details>


### [204] [The Long Road to the Same Answer: Cognitive Bias Under Escalating Reasoning Budgets in Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.10049)
*Obada Kraishan*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 通过30个情境题、6类认知偏差和12,350次API调用，研究发现推理模型并不比同源非推理模型更少偏差，且增加的思维量并未可靠降低偏差，甚至使评分方向进一步偏离人类方向。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 若推理模型的“深思熟虑”符合双加工理论，延长思考应削弱快速直觉判断产生的经典决策偏差；作者希望检验测试时推理是否构成理性保障。

Method: 使用覆盖锚定、框架、损失厌恶、承诺升级、可得性、确认六类偏差的30个情境题；在四个模型家族中，将每个推理模型与匹配的非推理版本配对；请求0、1,024、4,096、8,192 token的思维上限，共12,350次API调用；以每次调用实际消耗的推理token作为剂量，进行剂量—反应研究。

Result: 推理模型并未比其非推理同源模型偏差更少，各家族点估计方向相反但项目层面汇总对比不显著（Delta=+0.031, t(29)=1.45, p=.157）；偏差幅度未随实际 deliberation 增加而可靠下降，无显著负斜率，部分情况下带符号得分更远离人类方向；锚定是唯一与人类方向一致的偏差（d=1.89），其余五类中四类在全部七个模型中反向，框架效应可靠反转，承诺升级、确认与损失厌恶呈方向性反转，可得性缺失；回答前重述锚点的一行指令降低了全部五个锚定题目的锚定效应，但未达显著（p=.057）。

Conclusion: 结果反对将测试时推理视为理性保证，主张对部署模型逐项审计偏差。

Abstract: Reasoning models allocate extra computation at inference time and present their answers as the product of deliberate thought. If this deliberation works the way dual-process accounts of human cognition suggest, longer thinking should weaken the classic decision biases that fast, intuitive judgment produces. Using 30 vignettes covering six biases (anchoring, framing, loss aversion, escalation of commitment, availability, confirmation) from an established benchmark, we run a dose-response study across four model families, pairing each reasoning model with a matched non-reasoning sibling and requesting thinking ceilings of 0, 1,024, 4,096, and 8,192 tokens, for 12,350 API calls. Because a requested ceiling is not the same as realized deliberation, we use the reasoning tokens each call consumed as the dose. First, reasoning models are not less biased than their siblings; the point estimate leans the other way in every family, but the item-level pooled contrast is not reliable (Delta = +0.031, t(29) = 1.45, p = .157). Second, bias magnitude does not reliably fall as realized deliberation grows: no slope is significantly negative, and where anything moves it is the signed score drifting further from the human direction. Third, anchoring is the only bias in the human direction (d = 1.89). Four of the other five lean the opposite way in all seven models; with five items per bias, that reversal is reliable for framing and directional for escalation of commitment, confirmation, and loss aversion, while availability is absent. A one-line instruction to restate the anchor before answering lowered anchoring on all five anchoring items, which no amount of additional thinking did, although the effect does not reach significance (p = .057). The results argue against treating test-time reasoning as a rationality guarantee and for auditing deployed models bias by bias.

</details>


### [205] [Cache the Encoder Within:Compact, Reusable Memory across LLM Queries](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.10058)
*Hanzuo Liu,Chunyu Liu,Chaofan Lin,Alex Lamb,Mingyu Gao*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: EncBank 将预训练大模型的下层复用为文档编码器，紧凑存储其输出供上层适配读取器使用，实现可复用计算与紧凑内存的结合。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 重复查询共享文档会产生冗余编码，而缓存模型状态又带来持久化存储开销，需在复用计算与控制存储成本之间取得平衡。

Method: 基于 CoMem 的中间状态接口，把预训练 LLM 的下层作为可复用文档编码器，并将其输出紧凑存储，供适配的上层读取器使用；在每个骨干网络内跨存储精度共享一个自蒸馏后缀适配器，无需针对量化专门重训练。

Result: 在三个 Qwen 骨干（不同规模，含全注意力与混合架构）的五个基准套件上，4-bit 存储使各基准聚合分数与原生精度 EncBank 相差不超过 1 分；固定 Qwen3-8B 负载下保留原生精度持久化 GPU 存储的 28.1%。原生精度对照相对同证据、同适配器的文本重放获得 1.40x 选定包预填充加速，RULER 精度损失 3.12 分；原生精度 Qwen3.8-27B 配置通过 Terminal-Bench 2.1 的 89 项任务中的 70 项。

Conclusion: EncBank 将可复用计算与紧凑内存相结合，但任务保真度与端到端收益仍取决于工作负载、准备成本和复用频率。

Abstract: Repeated queries over shared documents incur redundant encoding, while caching model states introduces persistent storage costs. Building on CoMem's intermediate-state interface, EncBank treats a pretrained LLM's lower layers as a reusable document encoder and compactly stores their outputs for an adapted upper-layer reader. A self-distilled suffix adapter is shared across storage precisions within each backbone, without quantization-specific retraining. Across five benchmark suites on three Qwen backbones spanning different sizes and full-attention and hybrid architectures, 4-bit storage keeps each reported benchmark aggregate within one score point of native-precision EncBank. In a fixed Qwen3-8B workload, it retains 28.1% of the native-precision persistent GPU store. Separate native-precision controls yield a 1.40x selected-pack prefill speedup over same-evidence, same-adapter text replay, at a 3.12-point RULER accuracy cost. A native-precision Qwen3.8-27B configuration also passes 70 of 89 Terminal-Bench 2.1 tasks. EncBank thus combines reusable computation with compact memory, while task fidelity and end-to-end benefits remain dependent on the workload, preparation costs, and reuse frequency.

</details>


### [206] [ExperienceIndex: Artifact-Grounded Memory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.10091)
*Peter Baile Chen,Geoffrey X. Yu,Xinming Liu,Samuel Madden,Dan Roth,Jacob Andreas,Doug Downey,Michael Cafarella*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出 ExperienceIndex，一种为 AI agent 设计的经验层，通过存储与复用基于 artifact 的经验（单 artifact 经验与 artifact 对经验）来提升知识密集型任务的答案质量与效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 知识密集型任务需在共享语料（如判例、科学文献）上反复推理作答。人类能自然积累关于 artifact 的经验，快速定位相关 artifact 集合；而现有 AI agent 缺乏此类记忆方案，导致答案质量低、在线成本高。已有记忆方法多聚焦用户偏好、事实属性或抽象推理模式，未能捕获持久的、artifact 特定的知识。

Method: ExperienceIndex 作为轻量中间件，从先前推理轨迹中提取两类互补经验：(i) 单 artifact 经验，总结某 artifact 对过往任务的贡献；(ii) artifact 对经验，编码过去推理中发现的结构性关系。并通过经验检索机制引导 agent 为新任务定位完整的相关 artifact 集合。

Result: 在多种语料与采用不同检索框架的 agentic 方案上取得一致提升：答案质量最高提升 11.0 分，在线费用最高降低 50.5%。并验证两项收益：(i) 跨任务泛化，文本到 SQL 任务积累的经验可迁移至同一 artifact 语料上的事实型问答任务；(ii) 师生学习，强模型的经验可助弱模型达到可比性能。

Conclusion: 以 artifact 为中心的经验层能有效弥补现有记忆方案的不足，在提升知识密集型任务答案质量的同时显著降低在线成本，并具备跨任务迁移与师生知识传递的潜力。

Abstract: Knowledge-intensive tasks require answering many questions by reasoning about a shared corpus of artifacts (e.g., court cases, or scientific literature). As humans interact with these corpora, they naturally accumulate experiential knowledge about artifacts, enabling them to quickly identify the complete set of relevant artifacts for each new task. However, existing AI agents lack appropriate memory solutions to build or reuse such artifact-grounded experience, leading to lower answer quality and higher online cost. Existing memory solutions extract and reuse information from prior task-solving traces, but they primarily focus on user preferences, factual attributes, or abstract reasoning patterns rather than persistent artifact-specific knowledge. We introduce ExperienceIndex, a novel experience layer for AI agents that captures and reuses knowledge about artifacts based on prior reasoning traces. ExperienceIndex stores two complementary forms of experience: (i) single-artifact experiences that summarize an artifact's contribution to prior tasks and (ii) artifact-pair experiences that encode structural relationships discovered during past reasoning. Integrated as lightweight middleware, ExperienceIndex uses an experience retrieval mechanism to guide agents toward the complete set of relevant artifacts for new tasks, improving both answer quality and efficiency. Across diverse corpora and agentic solutions with different search frameworks, ExperienceIndex delivers consistent gains, raising answer quality by up to 11.0 points and reducing online dollar cost by up to 50.5%. We further demonstrate two benefits: (i) cross-task generalization, where experiences accumulated from text-to-SQL tasks transfer to factoid QA tasks over the same artifact corpus, and (ii) teacher-student learning, where experiences from a stronger model enable a weaker model to reach comparable performance.

</details>


### [207] [I would rather quit NLP than read another paper like this: The rise of antithesis in NLP papers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.10092)
*Olga Zamaraeva,Adrián Gude,Roi Santos-Ríos,Carlos Gómez-Rodríguez*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本研究考察学术写作中"rather than"结构（否定对比句式）的滥用现象：2026年ACL类论文中的使用率是2019年的7倍，GPT生成论文中更高；盲评标注者认为约十分之一的2026年用例"令人反感"，而2019年几乎为零；偏好数据评分者与开源奖励模型偏好该结构，诚实性指令也会促进其使用，作者推测这是基于成对偏好进行后训练的副作用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 近期LLM被广泛用于科学写作，许多研究者注意到模型会在论文中填充大量不必要的"反义对比"（antithesis），反复陈述工作"不做什么"，这类表达无助于提升精确性或表达质量，反而令审稿人反感而非留下好印象。作者希望系统研究这一现象——"rather than"结构的使用——的规模、来源与成因。

Method: 对比分析三类语料中"rather than"结构的使用：(1) 2019年ACL论文；(2) 2026年ACL风格arXiv论文；(3) 使用相同标题与摘要由GPT模型撰写的论文。统计该结构出现频率；由两名标注者在不知来源的条件下判断用例是否"令人反感"（annoying）；比较2009年与2026年的反感率及标注者间一致性；分析"令人反感"用例与正当用例在呈现被拒替代方案时的差异；并测试偏好数据评分者、开源奖励模型及"诚实"指令对该结构的倾向。

Result: 2026年该结构出现率为2019年的7倍，GPT论文中更高。两名标注者对2019年用例几乎不感到反感，但约有十分之一的2026年用例被判定为反感；两人的判断一致性较低，但约一半2026年论文含有至少一处令各自反感的使用。"令人反感"的用例比正当用例更倾向于贬低被拒绝的替代方案。偏好数据评分者和开源奖励模型偏好该结构，"诚实"指令也会促进其使用。

Conclusion: 作者推测"rather than"结构的泛滥是基于成对偏好（pairwise preferences）进行后训练的副作用：这类训练会奖励单条回复中的"自我否定/disavowal"，却无法感知该表达在整篇文本中累积的代价。换言之，偏好优化在局部层面鼓励了这类表达，但忽视了其在长文本语境中的负面影响。

Abstract: For better or worse, LLMs are by now used routinely for scientific writing.\footnote{This paper is no exception; we did use AI to assist with writing some of the sections (see Acknowledgments).} Many have noticed that recent models fill papers with unnecessary antithesis, stating over and over what the work does not do, in ways that do not contribute to its precision or quality of expression and annoy reviewers \emph{rather than impressing them}. We study the construction \emph{rather than} in ACL papers from 2019, ACL-style arXiv papers from 2026, and papers written by GPT models from the same titles and abstracts. Its rate in 2026 is seven times the 2019 rate, and higher still in the GPT papers. Two annotators, blind to the source, find almost no 2019 use \emph{annoying} and about one in ten 2026 uses; they seldom agree on which, yet about half of 2026 papers contain a use that annoys each of them. \emph{Annoying} uses present the rejected alternative less favorably than legitimate uses.
